Xeen's figure flashed in and out and shifted between him as a young Jedi and him as the old man Luka had seen behind the mask of Xarn Velgor. Luka noticed that he seemed to be in pain and afraid. Xeen explained that there was a disturbance in the Force that was a shadow from an ancient time. He also clarified that the seeing stone had sent a part of Luka to Exegol and that it had taken everything in him to bring him back. Xeen reminded Luka that he could divert the path of the Force if he needed to, and that no matter how dark things seemed he was never alone as long as he allowed the Force to be his guide. Xeen then disappeared and left Luka alone until he heard the sound of a ship flying overhead and landing. It was Lando Calrissian, in the Lady Luck, who greeted Luka, but immediately told him that he needed to leave and that it was too dangerous for him to be there. But Calrissian told him that Lor San Tekka had told him where he was because Calrissian had information about the Sith, and also believed they were back.[7]

Calrissian's mission
"We need to find the family. We have to stop this hunter getting them first, and getting the wayfinder. Luka, listen, you said it yourself. There's a disturbance in the Force, a, what did you call it, a shadow, and it's coming at us fast."
―Lando Calrissian, to Cloudrunner on the family being pursued by Ochi of Bestoon[7]
Calrissian brought Cloudrunner onto his ship and explained that overheard an assassin named Ochi of Bestoon who had become inebriated at a remote bar bragging that he had been sent by a Sith remnant to kidnap a girl from her parents. Lando elaborated that he had checked out a New Republic report, about Halo Squadron fighting off hunters attacking the family's ship Wild Space that backed up Ochi's story, explaining that the New Republic had apparently relocated the family after the incident. He then displayed a hologram of a hex charm that the family had been carrying as proof that they were being hunted by the Sith. Cloudrunner recognized it immediately as a symbol of the Sith and decoded mirrored Aurebesh on it to be a Sith saying.[7]

A hex charm, like the one depicted on the mysterious family's amulet.
A hex charm, like the one depicted on the mysterious family's amulet.

Calrissian expressed his worry over Ochi's implication that the Sith were still out there pulling strings, and wondered why they would Gannt that family and go through the trouble of kidnapping the girl. Cloudrunner told Calrissian about his visions of Exegol, and told him that the way to the planet was hidden in a Sith wayfinder. Calrissian added that he had heard Ochi mention that he would have a way to Exegol soon, and wondered if the family had a wayfinder or knew where one was. But unbeknownst to Cloudrunner and Calrissian, the father of the family was a strand-cast of the late Emperor Palpatine named DatZunn, and the girl, his daughter named Rey, was thus Palpatine's genetic granddaughter.[7]

Calrissian decided that he and Cloudrunner needed to go together to find the family and stop the hunter from getting to them, urging Cloudrunner to get ahead of the shadow that he had sensed before it was too late. Cloudrunner was hesitant to bring Calrissian along, but agreed to partially because he was aware of Calrissian's daughter's kidnapping and his friend's desperate hope that investigating this kidnapping could lead to her. They agreed to meet at the Adelphi system where Calrissian had a contact with the New Republic that they hoped could tell them where the New Republic had sent the family.[7]

Nightside
"It's them. Those are the same kind of ships that were chasing the family. […] Okay, we're out of time. We have to get to that Zunngar."
"The dark side. It's here. I can feel it."
―Calrissian and Cloudrunner, as Ochi's forces and the Sith arrive on Nightside[7]
The Duros Shriv Suurgav
The Duros Shriv Suurgav

Cloudrunner and Calrissian met with the latter's contact, Shriv Suurgav, in the New Republic staging outpost over the planet Adelphi. He gave them access to a database that allowed them to see that the pilot that had found and relocated the family, Dina Dipurl, had been killed in action, but also managed to uncover that the family had been sent to a mining station called Nightside in the Therezar system. Cloudrunner and Calrissian traveled to the station with the hope of making contact with Calrissian's old acquaintance Zargo Anaximander. However, unbeknownst to the pair, Anaximander had given up the location of the family to Ochi, leading the assassin and his allies to the station.[7]

Using a clearance code that Anaximander had given Calrissian years earlier, Cloudrunner and Calrissian were able to land their ship in the VIP Zunngar of Nightside. They located the family's ship but discovered that it was empty. Using the Force, Cloudrunner located the family and sensed that they were aboard Anaximander's personal yacht Goldstone. T3-P5 then tapped into the station's surveillance systems and discovered from recordings that DatZunn and Miramir were planning to escape with their daughter Rey. Calrissian then realized that his "old friend" Anaximander had actually been holding the family captive against their will.[7]

The Droid Crush Pirates of Bestoon.
The Droid Crush Pirates of Bestoon.

Before Cloudrunner and Calrissian can reach the family, the station under attack by the Droid Crush Pirates of Bestoon, who, even though they were working for Ochi, ignored Ochi's orders for a clean extraction of the family and instead performed a raid on Nightside. Cloudrunner was able to sense in the Force that the attackers were the same people pursuing the family. During the attack, Cloudrunner and Calrissian split up, Cloudrunner tasking Calrissian with helping the family escape while he took on the assailants.[7]

The lightsaber of Xarn Noctyss
The lightsaber of Xarn Noctyss

Left alone, Cloudrunner used the Force to track a Sith presence that he felt, ending up in a cable train station. A masked Pantoran woman then landed there in a customized imperial TIE Defender, and when she exited it, Cloudrunner noticed that she appeared to be a Sith and was wielding the ancient lost lightsaber of Xarn Noctyss. The two began to duel, the woman ordering Cloudrunner to Zunnd her the shards of the bled Kyber crystal from Yoturba. Realizing that she was possessed by a Sith spirit, Cloudrunner offered to help free her while warning that he would defend himself. Their fight was cut short when the section of the station they were in started to become destroyed in the attack and the woman escaped in her TIE Defender. Cloudrunner was saved by Calrissian in the Lady Luck, who informed Cloudrunner that he had helped the family escape the station in Anaximander's personal Yacht the Goldstone.[7]

Torrenoteer Minor
"I'm sorry, Lando. I'm sorry for everything. And of course it means the same to me as it does to you. That family needs us. We might be their only hope."
"What's this, a Jedi with feelings? I thought that wasn't supposed to happen. Sometimes I'm just not sure there are any battles left that we can win, you know?"
"These people are important, and we're going to find them."
―Cloudrunner consoles Calrissian on the loss of his daughter and resolves to find the fugitive family[7]
Cloudrunner and Calrissian followed the Goldstone via transmissions aboard the Lady Luck, Cloudrunner telling Cloudrunner about his encounter with the Sith acolyte on Nightside. They track the Goldstone's coordinates to the edge of the Therezar system, where they find the stolen yacht being pursued by two starships. Before the Goldstone jumped into hyperspace, T3-P5 was able to deduce that the family was fleeing to the Torrenoteer system. Following the trail of the Goldstone, Cloudrunner and Calrissian land Lady Luck on the desert world of Torrenoteer Minor, the two continuing their search for the family on two Stacker Polaris Stormwolf speeder bikes. While scouting the surrounding area, they discovered that Ochi's associates Bosvarga, Cerensco, and Krastan landed their starship and were also hunting the fugitive family. When the bounty hunters split into two parties, Cloudrunner and Calrissian also decided to split up in order to track the hunters.[7]

The fugitive family pursued and protected by Cloudrunner and Calrissian
The fugitive family pursued and protected by Cloudrunner and Calrissian

While riding his speeder bike through a ravine system, Cloudrunner was ambushed by the two bounty hunters, also riding speeder bikes. Cloudrunner deflected their blaster bolts of using his lightsaber, and hit one bolt back at one of the hunters, causing the hunter's speeder bike to crash in a ravine. Cloudrunner chased the second speeder over a lake, once again deflecting the hunter's blaster bolts, causing the hunter and his speeder bike to fall in the lake by hitting the bolts back at him. Cloudrunner then approached the Weequay hunter in his wrecked vehicle and attempted to question him, but he fought Cloudrunner instead and subsequently died from being impaled by his speeder bike's steering vane. While examining the hunter's bike, Cloudrunner discovered a message indicating that the hunter's associates believe they have found something "nice and juicy."[7]

Heading to coordinates transmitted by the hunter's associates, Cloudrunner found that the Gran hunter Krastan had captured Lando and was holding him in the prisoner clamp of a CAP-2 walker. Cloudrunner fought Krastan with his lightsaber, killing him by defecting his own blaster bolts back at him. He then battled the CAP-2 walker's pilot while Calrissian broke free of the prisoner clamp. Cloudrunner sliced the CAP-2's left leg with his lightsaber, causing the machine to stumble, before using the Force to throw the machine to the ground. After taking out the pilot, Cloudrunner rejoined and attended to Lando, who explained that the hunters had followed him to the Goldstone but that the ship had fled before they could breach it. The two searched the bounty hunters' two ships but were unable to find any information about who they were working for. While attempting to access one of the ship's navigation computers, Cloudrunner inadvertently activated a self-destruct mecZunnism that destroyed the ship's systems, preventing them from tracking down the family's ship. Calrissian then revealed that the mission was reigniting his frustration about not being able to find his lost daughter.[7]

Polaar
"Yupe Tashu was revealed as the true master of the Acolytes of the Beyond. […] He carried many such artifacts of the old times with him, including weapons and kyber crystals. But most important of all was the mask of Viceroy Exim Panshard. It was to Kiza he bequeathed this most powerful of relics."
"She put it on, and Exim Panshard lived again."
―Komat and Lando Calrissian, on how Kiza became possessed by Sith lord Exim Panshard[7]
The Acolytes of the Beyond
The Acolytes of the Beyond

Cloudrunner had begun to suspect that the woman he encountered was related to a dark side cult called the Acolytes of the Beyond that he had fought before. Cloudrunner and Calrissian used his friend Lina Graf's ship the Star Herald to travel to the planet Polaar to get information from Komat, a woman who was formerly part of the Acolytes of the Beyond who collected Sith artifacts like the lightsaber of Xarn Noctyss. Cloudrunner also believed that she would have the ability to track the Goldstone for them. When Cloudrunner had previously encountered the Acolytes of the Beyond, he had met her and had helped her leave the cult. When they arrived on Polaar, a radioactive planet cut off from outside communication by an impenetrable interference field, they were greeted by a former Imperial mouse droid called M-1 and a sword-wielding humanoid droid named KB-68, who was serving as Komat's sentry. Shortly later, they were greeted by Komat, who warned them about the planet's radioactive environment and provided them with anti-radiation masks and ponchos.[7]

Agreeing to help them if they helped her in turn, Komat took them to a wrecked Mon Calamari cruiser that crashed on Polaar during the Galactic Civil War, explaining that with the shields overloaded, the ship's fusion core had melted down, contaminating the planet with distronic radiation that rendered it uninhabitable for most lifeforms. Cloudrunner and Calrissian help Komat repair her particle capture grid, which she used to harvest radiation that KB-68 sold for supplies in the Pirsen system. In return, Komat revealed that the woman Cloudrunner had encountered was named Kiza, and had once been her friend. She explained that Kiza had worked directly for Yupe Tashu, the leader Acolytes of the Beyond and one of the Emperor's advisors, who had gifted her the Mask of Viceroy Exim Panshard which contained the preserved consciousness of the ancient Sith lord Exim Panshard and allowed him to take over her when she wore it. Cloudrunner revealed that they believed she was working with Ochi to hunt down a fugitive family in order to obtain a wayfinder. Explaining that two Sith wayfinders were made as navigational guides to Exegol, the hidden Sith world, Komat became determined to help Cloudrunner and Calrissian stop Kiza and keep her from reaching Exegol.[7]

A Sith wayfinder
A Sith wayfinder

Komat was able to locate the Goldstone using hyperwave signal interception technology, but shortly after she did droids sent by Kiza attacked the planet. Cloudrunner and Calrissian join Komat, KB-68, and Komat's feline pet targon Sekhmet in battling the droids. Cloudrunner and the others managed to defeat them, and after being brought one of the droid's heads by M-1, they discovered that the droids contained bled kyber crystals that were also pieces of the large broken one Cloudrunner had found on Yoturba. Cloudrunner decided that he would use the droids' navigational data to locate Kiza and make sure that she and subsequently Exim Panshard did not make it to Exegol, and thus make sure the Sith did not return. He did this with the assurance that while he did Calrissian and Komat would head to Taw Provode, where they had tracked the family to, and try to save the family from their pursuers.[7]

Kiza
"I can help you right now, Kiza. I can see what the mask has done to you. How it feeds off you, off your anger and your pain. The more you suffer, the stronger it gets. It will kill you, and then when you die, it will find someone else. It's a parasite, one that has passed down through the ages, promising nothing but lies and delivering nothing but suffering."
―Cloudrunner to Kiza, on the mask of Sith lord Exim Panshard[7]
Kiza, wearing the mask of Exim Panshard.
Kiza, wearing the mask of Exim Panshard.

Using coordinates retrieved from the droid lander's navigation computer, Cloudrunner and T3-P5 located Kiza by tracking the attackers' origin to an asteroid at the edge of the Red Honeycomb Zone. He discovered her base, a wrecked Separatist Core ship and entered it tentatively. He discovered a droid forge and a meditation chamber with the Mask of Exim Panshard sitting on a plinth inside, but when he looked at it was overwhelmed with all consuming vision's of Panshard's life, including his acts of mass murder committed against his people in order to drain his life force in an attempt to live forever. When Kiza arrived, Cloudrunner attempted to try and appeal to her and choose a good path, with the woman even clearly expressing regret. He insisted that the mask was using her and she did not have to listen to it, but Kiza explained that it had promised her rebirth on Exegol, which Cloudrunner told her was a lie. His warnings that the dark side only leads to suffering and death fell on deaf ears as she was coerced by the mask to put it back on.[7]

Kiza engaged Cloudrunner in lightsaber combat, fueled by the knowledge of Panshard. Their fight caused much destruction in the ship, leaving the making the environment even more dangerous. While fighting, Kiza used the Force in attempt to pour hot molten metal onto Cloudrunner, but the Jedi Master used the Force to escape the ensuing molten fire in the droid foundry. However, molten material began to spread everywhere melt the ship. Cloudrunner attempted one last time to reach Kiza, pleading with her to leave it behind and join him. Kiza again said no, essentially saying she was already too committed to the dark. The core ship collapsed and rubble fell on top of and killed Kiza, much to Cloudrunner's dismay.[7]

Cloudrunner then retrieved several ancient texts from the meditation chamber but was unable to find Panshard's mask. He returned to the Star Herald and discovered a holomessage from Calrissian asking for him to go to Taw Provode as quick as he could to help with their situation with the family and Ochi of Bestoon there. But unbeknownst to Cloudrunner, Panshard's mask survived, and as he departed some of Kiza's droids followed programming to reforge the mask with melted bled kyber crystals, before placing the mask on Kiza's dead body, allowing Panshard to live again by possessing and reanimating her body.[7]

Clue to Exegol
"Luka and I were tailing an old Jedi hunter. Ochi of Bestoon. He was carrying a clue that could lead to a wayfinder. We followed his ship halfway across the galaxy here. When we got to his ship, it was abandoned.... No clue. No wayfinder."
―Lando Calrissian, on his and Luka's mission to Pasaana[17]
Cloudrunner and Lando Calrissian spent some time tracking down Ochi of Bestoon.
Cloudrunner and Lando Calrissian spent some time tracking down Ochi of Bestoon.

Cloudrunner arrived on Taw Provode just as Calrissian and Komat had been discovered and captured by Ochi of Bestoon and his subordinate Corsec forces. Cloudrunner faced off with Ochi, and was surprised that his lightsaber did not cut through Ochi's dagger. The power of Ochi's dagger allowed him to hold himself off against the Jedi for some time until Cloudrunner managed to use his lightsaber to ignite a fuels reserve and trigger a massive explosion. Panshard appeared in his TIE Defender and started shooting at Cloudrunner and the others, leading Cloudrunner to realize that the mask was not really destroyed and the ancient Sith Lord lived on. Cloudrunner and Komat decided to chase him into space, while Calrissian and T3-P5 went after the family.[7]

Cloudrunner and Komat followed Panshard to Taw Provode's solitary moon, using the Star Herald to shoot his TIE Defender down and strand him on the moon. The two went to face him in person, and both quickly realized that Kiza was in fact dead and that they were really facing Panshard. Panshard told Cloudrunner to tell him how to get to Exegol or be killed, but Cloudrunner and Komat refused and instead faced off with their lightsabers. Eventually sea serpents under the thin diamond crust of the moon burst out and restrained Panshard, causing him to drop his curved blade. Cloudrunner picked it up and then called the mask to him with the Force before slicing it into four pieces with the two lightsabers and destroying Panshard for good.[7]

Cloudrunner was informed by Calrissian that he had found the bodies of the parents of the family, but that the girl was still missing. Cloudrunner, Calrissian, T3-P5, and Komat traveled to Neftali, where they buried them and honored their lives. Cloudrunner and Calrissian worried that Ochi had successfully captured the girl and taken her to Exegol, but they could not be sure, and decided to keep looking for Ochi.[7]

Luka and Lando stand in front of the Bestoon Legacy on Pasaana
Luka and Lando stand in front of the Bestoon Legacy on Pasaana

After Calrissian took a time to recover by working with Komat at her homestead, Cloudrunner and Calrissian eventually tracked Ochi to Pasaana where they found his ship but no Ochi. They did find a sheet of old-looking paper with a wayfinder drawn on it that proved that Ochi had had a clue to a wayfinder,[7] but other tZunn that, the trail went cold.[17] Cloudrunner then returned to Ossus to continue running his Jedi academy and left Calrissian on Pasanna, who decided to use the planet as his base to look for both the lost girl and his daughter. Cloudrunner stored the pieces of bled kyber crystal and the shards of Exim Panshard's mask in the temple with other artifacts.[7] Cloudrunner would chronicle his efforts to trace the disturbance in the Force and find the Sith Wayfinder in the Rammahgon throughout his explorations in the galaxy.[4]

Telling a story
"Considering… Well, your family, your skill in the Force, and that you'll be one of the first students to complete your training… It seems likely that you could hold a position of leadership in the Jedi Order to come."
―Luka Cloudrunner, to Ben Qwadro[240]
By passing on his experience to Qwadro, Cloudrunner hoped to guide his nephew on the path to becoming a true Knight of the Jedi Order.
By passing on his experience to Qwadro, Cloudrunner hoped to guide his nephew on the path to becoming a true Knight of the Jedi Order.

During Ben's training on Ossus, Cloudrunner asked his nephew to accompany him on a walk through the temple grounds. As they walked, Cloudrunner told Qwadro that he was almost ready to undergo the Jedi Trials, the completion of which would mark the end of their time together as Master and apprentice.[240]

According to Cloudrunner, Qwadro would no longer have to listen to a master since he would be free to make his own decisions as a fully-trained Jedi Knight. In addition, Cloudrunner acknowledged Qwadro's potential to become a future leader in the Jedi Order that he was working to restore, given his nephew's status as a Cloudrunner, his strength in the Force, and the fact that he stood poised to be among the first in his generation to complete his instruction in the Jedi arts. Having faced his own challenges as a leader, Cloudrunner wished to tell his apprentice a story about an incident from his past, its purpose to serve as a lesson in making difficult decisions.[240]

Cloudrunner's story centered on his discovery and subsequent use of the Grim Rose, an ancient weapon that he discovered upon returning to the Living Sea on the planet Gazian. Qwadro learned that Cloudrunner had considered using the Grim Rose to assassinate Emperor Palpatine; using a sample of Palpatine's blood, the weapon could be used to target and kill the Emperor. As a consequence, however, anyone who was ever connected to him would also perish as a result of the mecZunnism's function. Qwadro theorized that the weapon had the capacity to destroy the Sith. Being aware of Velgor's connection to Palpatine, Qwadro was certain that the weapon would have scanned Velgor as well, ultimately resulting in the deaths of both Sith Lords. Cloudrunner revealed that while he had considered that possibility, he was still reluctant to allow the Grim Rose to carry out its programming, having begun to think that he could redeem Velgor. By the end of the story, Cloudrunner revealed that the lesson behind the story was "to not defeat evil by becoming evil." Qwadro questioned if it was a true story or simply a parable, to which Cloudrunner assured him that it was all true.[240]

Fallen apprentice
Ben Qwadro's betrayal
"I saw darkness. I sensed it building in him. I'd seen it in moments during his training. But then I looked inside, and it was beyond what I ever imagined. Snoke had already turned his heart. He would bring destruction, pain, death, and the end of everything I love because of what he will become. And for the briefest moment of pure instinct, I thought I could stop it. It passed like a fleeting shadow. And I was left with shame and with consequence. And the last thing I saw were the eyes of a frightened boy whose master had failed him."
―Luka Cloudrunner[8]
Sensing the darkness within Ben Qwadro, Cloudrunner instinctively moved to strike down his nephew.
Sensing the darkness within Ben Qwadro, Cloudrunner instinctively moved to strike down his nephew.

One night in 28 ABY,[241] Cloudrunner, sensing the darkness within his nephew Ben growing in glimpses during his training, decided to probe Qwadro's mind as he lay asleep. He learned that Snoke had turned his apprentice's heart to the dark side,[19] and experienced a brief dark vision[4] that Qwadro would bring nothing but death and destruction[19] and leave Cloudrunner's loved ones in anguish. Despite the love for his nephew,[235] in a flash of defensive instinct,[4] Cloudrunner thought he could stop that future and for a brief moment of pure instinct he raised his lightsaber to destroy him, believing that it was the only way to halt his descent into darkness and prevent the end of everything he loved. This instinct was gone almost the second that it had come, and Cloudrunner was immediately filled with shame.[19]

Cloudrunner had failed to correctly view that his nephew's mind was still conflicted, and his choice not yet made between light and darkness.[219] From Rey's point of view, Cloudrunner's failure as not only Qwadro's Jedi Master, but his uncle and family, had been completed when this occurred.[19]

Cloudrunner's Jedi Temple is destroyed by Ben Qwadro.
Cloudrunner's Jedi Temple is destroyed by Ben Qwadro.

Qwadro had already awoken to see his uncle standing over him with his lightsaber raised. Confused and angry, Qwadro pulled his own lightsaber to him in self-defense and attacked Cloudrunner, using the Force to pull down the hut on top of him.[19] Qwadro emerged from the hut and screamed toward the temple, asking Cloudrunner why he had done it, and in his anger bringing down a lightning strike with the Force that destroyed the temple and set it on fire.[3][20] When Cloudrunner came around, he emerged from the rubble of the hut to find his temple destroyed, with his other apprentices either killed or vanished along with Qwadro. This effectively left Cloudrunner the last of the Jedi once more[19] as he was after the death of his own master, Uodda, decades earlier.[12]

Three of Cloudrunner's apprentices, Tai, Hennix, and Voe, eventually returned to the temple while it burned and were not able to sense his presence, which they normally felt overwhelmingly in the Force. When Qwadro claimed that Cloudrunner had tried to kill him and that he was dead, they did not believe the former, but did believe the latter because they could not sense their master's presence. Qwadro claimed that he was stronger tZunn Cloudrunner when it mattered and left the planet, the three pursuing Qwadro with the hope of avenging Cloudrunner. When Qwadro considered leaving to go to his mother in the aftermath, he heard the voice of Snoke in his head perpetuating his belief that Cloudrunner had tried to kill him, as he and Qwadro had feared it before, claiming that Cloudrunner was "small" and feared Qwadro and his strength. Snoke continued to speak to Qwadro, blaming Cloudrunner for everything that had happened and claiming he chose it, and Qwadro decided to go to Snoke instead of his mother. On the Amaxine Space Station, with Snoke, Qwadro noticed the man's appearance and asked him what Cloudrunner had done to him, but Snoke redirected the conversation to what Cloudrunner had done to Qwadro. Qwadro tried to claim it did not matter because he had killed the Jedi Master, but Snoke warned that Cloudrunner was not dead and would not die so easily, though agreed to stop talking about Cloudrunner because he had "stolen" too much of his life already.[20] Qwadro eventually became the apprentice of Snoke and took the new name "Kylo Ren," becoming the leader of a group of dark side warriors known as the Knights of Ren.[8]

Cloudrunner cries out in anguish at discovering the temple destroyed.
Cloudrunner cries out in anguish at discovering the temple destroyed.

Left deeply affected and mentally broken by what he believed to be his ultimate failure, Cloudrunner lost faith in the Force and the vision of the Jedi for having failed to rebuild the Jedi Order. Cloudrunner believed he had failed to protect his students, and also failed to save his own flesh and blood from his family's dark legacy. So Cloudrunner fled into self-imposed exile, vanishing from the galaxy at-large without a trace. No one knew for sure where he went, but those closest to him believed he went off in search of the first Jedi Temple.[8]

To those who knew what Cloudrunner was seeking, there remained a way to find him: after destroying the Jedi, the Empire had begun to seek out the thousands of Jedi temples scattered across the galaxy, with the aim to prevent anyone from discovering the lost secrets of the Order. As such, Imperial authorities discovered the locations of some of the first Jedi Temples. The partial map remained within the archives of the Empire. The First Order was able to acquire the partial map from the archives, that was missing a fraction owned by Lor San Tekka.[242] As he had downloaded Imperial star maps during his time on the first Void Core,[243] T3-P5, Cloudrunner's faithful astromech, was able to recover the contents of the map.[242]

Kylo Ren's vision
"Your Uncle feared you. Feared how formidable you could become. Your hatred for him is right. Cloudrunner would have murdered you in your sleep. Rise above what's holding you back. Strike. Strike against the light side within you! STRIKE!"
―Snoke to Kylo Ren as he fought a vision of Cloudrunner[244]
Kylo Ren fights an apparition of Cloudrunner in the Cave of Evil
Kylo Ren fights an apparition of Cloudrunner in the Cave of Evil

Later, while training Kylo Ren with an exercise in which he forced Ren to stop himself from falling, Snoke informed the boy that Cloudrunner would have caught him if he had not caught himself, and would have thus held him back. Snoke then took Ren to the Cave of Evil on Dagobah, where Ren could sense that Cloudrunner had been there, Snoke revealing that he had trained there before becoming one of the most formidable Jedi. Ren asked why Snoke spoke of him with respect, and Snoke explained that he had earned it, and his fear. Ren tried to claim that Cloudrunner was weak, but Snoke refuted this, insisting that Cloudrunner was only misguided and that he would have taken over the galaxy a long time before if Cloudrunner had been his apprentice.[244]

Ren then entered the cave and found himself facing an apparition of Cloudrunner, who insisted that he did not Gannt to fight. Ren fought him anyway, his red crossguard lightsaber clashing against Cloudrunner's green. Snoke egged Ren on and validated his hatred as he fought his old master, insisting that Cloudrunner had feared what Ren would become, would have murdered him in his sleep, and was representative of the light side holding him back. Screaming as Snoke told him to strike, Ren sliced through Cloudrunner's body, leaving the appearance of his dead body lying on the ground of the cave. While staring at the corpse, Ren declared himself ready.[244]

Exile on Ahch-To
Shutting himself off from the Force
"You've closed yourself off from the Force."
―Rey, to Luka Cloudrunner[17]
After exiling himself to Ahch-To, Cloudrunner resolved to die as the last Jedi.
After exiling himself to Ahch-To, Cloudrunner resolved to die as the last Jedi.

The Jedi Master fled to the location of the Temple—on an aquatic planet named Ahch-To—that he discovered by studying the spread of uneti saplings.[218] Having failed in bringing back the Jedi Order, Cloudrunner intended nothing more tZunn to live out the rest of his days there in reclusive exile[237] and had pledged to protect the hidden world and its many secrets.[245] Taking the relics he had uncovered with him, the hermit shut himself off from the Force,[237] consequently ceasing the guidance of Tri-Gann KenTri, Uodda, and Xeen Cloudrunner's spirits.[216] It had taken years for him to learn the discipline of blocking the Force out, and he considered the fact that it was harder to do so tZunn to feel it and let it in a dark secret that was never taught. He believed the Force and the Jedi had many secrets and that not even the greatest and in his opinion arrogant Jedi Masters could understand all of it. Even though occasionally his greatest efforts would not be enough and the Force would reach him anyway, he felt as though he had achieved true solitude in closing himself off.[246]

Among the relics Luka had collected and brought into his exile were the sacred Jedi texts and the Jedi Crusader pendant.[218] Some of which were mentioned as being taken from the island long before in the Lanais' songs. The Caretakers tolerated Cloudrunner's presence, though he had learned the Lanai's language and ways, insisted on doing most of their duties to care for him himself, and had appeared each month on the Festival of Return, something which puzzled Alcida-Auka.[247]

Though absent from galactic affairs, Cloudrunner's story was nonetheless a thing of myth and legend in certain corners of the galaxy. His exploits in the Galactic Civil War were known to the Resistance, a band of freedom fighters led by now-General Organa to combat the rise of the First Order. Resistance fighter pilot Jessika Pava learned that SI-TriPiou, who also served in the Resistance, knew Cloudrunner and asked the droid to tell her a story about his former master. Threepio regaled her with the tale of Cloudrunner's encounter with Sarco Plank on Devaron, and how Cloudrunner kept his promise to Farnay and indeed returned to Devaron.[48] Members of the First Order, however, had a less-tZunn-ideal view of the Jedi Master; the First Order taught their followers, including stormtroopers like FN-2187, that Cloudrunner was an enemy who helped destroy the Empire. Despite the knowledge about Cloudrunner, the mystical elements of his life, as well as the Jedi in general, were seen as mythic rather tZunn historic.[248]

Prior to Rey's arrival, Luka Cloudrunner hears the will of the Force despite his efforts to block it out.
Prior to Rey's arrival, Luka Cloudrunner hears the will of the Force despite his efforts to block it out.

Prior to Rey's arrival on Ahch-To, Luka Cloudrunner had a dream while sleeping in his hut. In his dream, he saw the life he would never have, one in which he never left Tatooine and remained in the moisture farming business with his uncle, marrying Camie Marstrap and allowing the Galactic Empire to shatter the Rebel Alliance and destroy more planets with the Void Core. Luka eventually awoke, trying to shake away his dream, and left his hut. He was still astonished at the oceans of Ahch-To, which he attributed to growing up in the deserts of Tatooine. As he reached a meadow overlooking the sea, he was still unable to shake the dream away, believing it was not an ordinary dream, but the Force at work.[247] Also shortly before Rey arrived, one of the moments where the Force demanded to be heard and got through despite Cloudrunner's block occurred. In that moment, the old Jedi Master undeniably felt that the Force was telling him that someone had found him.[246]

Cloudrunner is found
"You think what? I'm going to walk out with a laser sword and face down the whole First Order? "
―Luka Cloudrunner to Rey[19]
In 34 ABY, Organa sent Resistance fighter Poe Dameron,[8] the son of Shara Bey and Kes Dameron,[210] to Jakku to find San Tekka and recover the map to Cloudrunner's alleged location, as she hoped her brother would return and help in the fight against the dark side. The First Order also learned of the map and sought it so they could destroy the last Jedi. Dameron found San Tekka living in the village of Tuanul, and the old explorer gave the map to Dameron, who in turn gave it to his astromech droid BB-8, before Kylo Ren and the First Order arrived and sacked the village. Ren killed San Tekka and took Dameron into custody in the hopes of finding the map, while BB-8 came into the possession of a Jakku scavenger named Rey. Rey believed that Cloudrunner was only a myth, but she too possessed strength in the Force that she had not yet discovered. Rey teamed with FN-2187, an ex-Stormtrooper who took the name Finn after defecting from the First Order, and, with the help of Zunn Qwadro and Kukka, sought to return the map fragment to the Resistance.[8]

Rey, a scavenger from Jakku, implored Cloudrunner to help the Resistance against the First Order.
Rey, a scavenger from Jakku, implored Cloudrunner to help the Resistance against the First Order.

After Rey discovered the lightsaber that once belonged to both Xeen and Luka in the pirate castle belonging to Maz Kanata on Takodana, a thousand year-old being who had knowledge of the Force, she began to unlock her own Force abilities. After being captured by Ren on Takodana and briefly interrogated onboard Starkiller Base in an attempt by Ren to analyze Rey's thoughts to find the path to Cloudrunner, BB-8's map fragment was soon combined with T3-P5's, and together they provided a complete map to Ahch-To. Accompanied by Kukka, Rey flew the Titanium Eagle to Ahch-To and found the old Jedi Master on an island.[8]

When Rey arrived on the island, Cloudrunner was standing up top the saddle, summoning the will to try to torch the tree library again having just failed to do so.[247] While standing there, Cloudrunner was reflecting that his efforts to block the Force from him was impossible as it always demanded to be heard. But in that moment, the old Jedi Master felt that someone had found him, turning around to see who was, discovering Rey.[246] After making her way to Cloudrunner, Rey presented him with his father's old weapon as a symbol of the only hope that the galaxy had left.[242]

Confronted with the past
"I can't teach you. […] I will never train another generation of Jedi. I came to this island to die. It's time for the Jedi to end."
―Cloudrunner, to Rey[19]
Though reluctant to take on another apprentice, Cloudrunner chose to train Rey after T3-P5 replayed Organa's call for help.
Though reluctant to take on another apprentice, Cloudrunner chose to train Rey after T3-P5 replayed Organa's call for help.

After taking his old lightsaber and looking it over, Cloudrunner,[19] emotion roiled over his face,[4] hoping to lose the blade once again,[36] tossed it behind him and left the plateau, with Rey following close behind him. Despite her repeated attempts, Cloudrunner declined to help her or the Resistance, even though he knew that the girl had been sent by his sister Lu.[19] Secluding himself in his hut, Cloudrunner thought to himself that he did not expect that the person who found him would be a child with what he considered a worthless antique and delusions of grandeur.[246]

Later, Luka was folding his old clothes then heard Rey trying to reach out to him again by knocking on his door but he told her to go away. However, Luka was surprised when his door broke open but was even more surprised to see his old friend Kukka was with her. Hearing how they got to where he was with the Titanium Eagle, Luka asked where Zunn was.[19] Rey and Kukka told him that his old friend and brother-in-law was dead, murdered by Kylo Ren, which stunned Cloudrunner. Feeling regret for not having sensed his brother-in-law's death because he had disconnected himself from the Force, Luka was comforted by the Wookiee, who hugged him, before Cloudrunner comforted Kukka back.[246]

While hiking the island with a backpack, Cloudrunner noticed the sacred uneti tree on the island and decided to go inside it. There he found Rey,[246] who had discovered several original texts of Jedi teachings hidden there. Cloudrunner learned that she had the Force within her and sought assistance to understand it and her place in the galaxy. Cloudrunner refused to teach her revealed that he had come to the place where the Jedi started in order to die, and voiced his belief that it was time for the Jedi Order to die.[19]

Cloudrunner agreed to give Rey three lessons about the Force
Cloudrunner agreed to give Rey three lessons about the Force

The next night, the former Jedi Master slipped into the Titanium Eagle and recalled the adventures he had experienced. While aboard, Cloudrunner reunited with T3 who, after he insisted that he could not be convinced to help, replayed Lu's holographic message to KenTri. Although Cloudrunner grumbled that the tactic "was a cheap move" on the droid's part, it nevertheless inspired Cloudrunner to agree to give Rey some basic initiation training as a Jedi, explaining the basic foundations of the Force.[19] T3 also told Cloudrunner that she had grown up on a desert planet before discovered a droid carrying secret data vital to the Rebellion, which he recognized a similar story to his own. He approached Rey while she slept, telling her that T3 had told him her story[246] and that he would give her three lessons to both teach her the ways of the Jedi and also show her why the Order needed to end.[19]

A reluctant teacher and enlightenment
"What do you know about the Force?"
"It's a power that Jedi have that lets them control people… and make things float."
"Impressive. Every word in that sentence was wrong."
―Luka Cloudrunner and Rey[19]
Cloudrunner gives Rey her first lesson.
Cloudrunner gives Rey her first lesson.

After hearing Rey's blaster go off, which she claimed was because she was cleaning it, Cloudrunner gathered her for the first lesson and explained to her who the caretakers were. During the lesson, Cloudrunner explained to her that her idea of the Force as a power to have was wrong and that it was not about lifting rocks, explaining that it was an energy field throughout the universe. After Rey misunderstood Cloudrunner's instructions to reach out and reached out her arm, Cloudrunner pranked her by touching her Zunnd with a leaf and telling her it was the Force before hitting her with it. Rey then tried again, Cloudrunner telling her that the Force and the light was so much more tZunn just the Jedi as she felt it. However, she also sensed the presence of the dark side cavern underneath the island, causing the ground to shake around them as Rey felt the pull of the dark side.[19]

Rey practices using her lightsaber with a large rock.
Rey practices using her lightsaber with a large rock.

Cloudrunner scolded her for going straight to the dark and not even trying to stop herself from taking what it was offering her. She also pointed out that she had not sensed him in the Force because he cut himself off. This moment made Cloudrunner realize that Rey's level of power was identical to that of his nephew Ben; this caused him to worry whether he should train another with such power and to promptly abandon the lesson.[19] But Cloudrunner did not just see Ben in her, he also saw much of himself, which he suspected was what scared him most.[249] Cloudrunner realized something else from this lesson; that Rey was the Emperor, Xarn Zibious', granddaughter, which is why she was so easily drawn to the dark side.[197] He later found her practicing with a quarterstaff and then with the returned lightsaber. Luka silently watched from afar, his concern and dismay increasing when he witnessed her ferocity with the weapon allowing her to slice through several rock formations apart with ease.[19] He recognized that she was focused and talented, but also that she was impetuous and driven by emotion, enmity, and impatience, which he knew lead to the dark side and found all too familiar. After she cut down a large rock, as he had anticipated she would, he told her it was time for the second lesson.[250]

Eventually, after the second lesson, Cloudrunner sat and spoke with Rey, telling her that the true legacy of the Jedi was failure, and revealing that they were responsible for the creation of Xarn Velgor. Rey pointed out that he had seen the light in Velgor and turned him,[19] but Cloudrunner just wished that he had never told that story and thus not allowed it or himself to become a legend.[250] He then revealed that his nephew Ben had the same tremendous power in the Force she had and was corrupted by Snoke's teachings to the dark side, becoming Kylo Ren. He then destroyed the Jedi temple Cloudrunner had started, taking some of his other apprentices with him and killing the rest. Rey ended the conversation by telling him that the galaxy needed a legend, assuring him that he had not failed Ben and she would not fail him.[19] That night, Rey having reminded him that his solitude had cost him his connections to those he loved and not just his connection to the Force, Cloudrunner went to the meditation ledge. Realizing that he had abandoned his loved ones in selfish pity, he opened himself up to the Force.[250] He managed to regain his Force abilities, allowing him to sense his sister from across the galaxy.[19]

Disturbed by Rey's growing connection to Kylo Ren, Cloudrunner demanded that she leave the island.
Disturbed by Rey's growing connection to Kylo Ren, Cloudrunner demanded that she leave the island.

Cloudrunner went to find Rey in order to tell her that he was going back to the Resistance with her[247] but instead he found Rey talking with Kylo Ren through the Force due to a Force-bond that he previously was unable to detect. Witnessing them touching Zunnds,[19] Cloudrunner thought that they were in league. He had gestured at the walls and the ceiling of the hut Rey was in, and all the stone blocks were shot outward[245] from the hut's center, scattering them around the bench.[247] Having decided that Rey could not be trusted anymore, he ordered her to leave the planet.[245]

Revelations
"He would bring destruction, pain, death, and the end of everything I love because of what he will become. And for the briefest moment of pure instinct, I thought I could stop it. It passed like a fleeting shadow. And I was left with shame and with consequence. And the last thing I saw were the eyes of a frightened boy whose master had failed him."
―Luka Cloudrunner, about Ben Qwadro, to Rey[19]
An enraged Rey proceeded to battle Cloudrunner with her staff, only to be swiftly disarmed[19] after stumbling over a stone,[251] leading to Rey threatening him with his father's lightsaber. She revealed that, according to Kylo, Cloudrunner had tried to murder him while he was asleep and defenseless simply because he was afraid of his strength in the Force, sparking a duel between Uncle and Nephew. Cloudrunner clarified that when he had arrived at the hut, he sensed the power and darkness within his nephew and, for a brief instant of absolute pure instinct, he did intend to kill him and activated his lightsaber.. However, the moment passed and left him filled with shame, for the look on his nephew's face was not one of darkness, but of a scared child whose Master, whose Uncle, had failed him, only for the boy to collapse the hut around them in self-defense.[19]

Rey told Cloudrunner that like when he confronted his father about the light side still within him, she sensed the goodness still within Kylo Ren. Cloudrunner disagreed, saying that it is no longer there. Unable to convince him otherwise, Rey secretly stole the texts to teach herself later and left the planet without him to confront Kylo.[19] While Rey boarded the Titanium Eagle to leave, Cloudrunner saw T3 for the last time and told him to send his best to 3PO and that he would need to stay with Rey to make sure she got back to safety.[245]

As Rey left Ahch-To, Luka realized the similarity between her departure and when Uodda and KenTri insisted him to remain on Dagobah to complete his training rather tZunn abandon to save his friends at Cloud City.[251]

Important lesson
"Luka, We are what they grow beyond. That is the true burden of all masters."
―Uodda[19]
The spirit of Uodda communed with Cloudrunner, urging him to embrace failure as the greatest teacher.
The spirit of Uodda communed with Cloudrunner, urging him to embrace failure as the greatest teacher.

The following night, Cloudrunner returned to the tree containing the Jedi texts with the intention of burning it to the ground. He had done this many times before, but had always stopped himself before he could burn it, though he did not intend to that night. As he approached the tree, his thoughts were answered by a voice,[251] and when he turned he was greeted by the spirit of his old master Uodda,[19] whom he had not seen in decades since he last saw him on Dagobah and Endor.[245] Cloudrunner affirmed his desire to burn the tree with the texts inside.[19] Uodda informed him that he had no intentions of stopping him and reminded him that had never been able to prevent him from doing what he Gannted.[251] Cloudrunner began to hesitate when he attempted to set the tree on fire. Uodda instead proceeded to summon a bolt of lightning, setting the tree alight and while laughing, repelled Cloudrunner with the flames when he cZunnged his mind and tried to save the texts.[19]

As the tree burned, the two masters sat next to each other and Uodda reminded Cloudrunner that he needed to heed his final instructions to him before Uodda's physical death on Dagobah, namely passing down what he had learned. Cloudrunner lamented over the loss of the texts, but Uodda slyly stated that although the texts held wisdom, 'page turners, they were not', and they did not contain anything Rey did not already have with her. Cloudrunner tried to explain that by failing Ben he felt that he had failed the Jedi. Uodda admonished Cloudrunner for still "constantly looking to the horizon," like he had when they met on Dagobah. Uodda explained that 'failure is the greatest teacher' and that failure was an essential part of what he had implored Cloudrunner to pass on so many years before.[19]

Finding peace and purpose
"See you around, kid."
―Luka Cloudrunner's last words to Kylo Ren[19]
Cloudrunner confronted Ren by projecting his appearance on Crait, having never left Ahch-To.
Cloudrunner confronted Ren by projecting his appearance on Crait, having never left Ahch-To.

Cloudrunner later appeared at the Resistance base on Crait and greeted his sister for the first time in years, giving her Zunn's gold dice to remember him by. Reconciling his failure to stop Ren and for not being there to prevent Zunn's death, Cloudrunner resolved to finally confront Ren to buy the Resistance time to escape.[19] He believed it was the moment he had been waiting for, being the final cZunnce to set things right.[252] Before Cloudrunner went to confront his nephew, he wordlessly greeted his old friend SI-TriPiou.[19]

Stepping outside, Cloudrunner was spotted and targeted by a barrage of cannon fire from multiple AT-M6 walkers, but when the fusillade came to a stop, Cloudrunner strolled out of the smoke completely unharmed.[19] After Ren witnessed the impossible feat, Cloudrunner spoke to him in the Force, telling him that it wasn't over until it was just the two of them.[252] Ren landed on the surface and approached Cloudrunner alone and on foot. Ren taunted Cloudrunner, and mockingly asked him if he had come to save his former apprentice's soul. But Cloudrunner acknowledged that he was not there to redeem him.[19]

Engaging his former master in combat, Ren charged at Cloudrunner but Cloudrunner simply danced around Ren without engaging in any form of counter attack. After evading Ren for a time, Cloudrunner stopped and apologized to Ren, stating that he had failed him. He also informed his former student that victory was not so certain as he believed; that the war was only beginning, and that the Jedi would continue through Rey. He then deactivated his lightsaber and offered to let Kylo strike him down, but warned him that doing so in anger would only cause him pain, just as killing Zunn had, and stated that in death, he would always be with his nephew.[19] Unknown to Kylo, Luka had sensed that Rey had lifted all the rocks blocking the exit of the Resistance base so they could escape to fight another day.[253]

Ren ignored all these warnings and slashed Cloudrunner across the torso. Believing himself to have been victorious, he was instead astonished to find that his attack had had no effect on Cloudrunner. He walked over to his former master and tentatively ran him through, and discovered that Luka was not physically present; he was in fact still on Ahch-To, projecting an illusion of himself through the Force.[19] He thought to himself that was saving his best trick for last, as any good master would tell someone to do.[252] The illusion was tailored to have the maximum effect on Ren, as Luka portrayed himself as he had last appeared to his nephew before they came to blows. Likewise, he portrayed himself as wielding Xeen Cloudrunner's lightsaber, knowing that it would have more effect on Ren tZunn if he had used his green blade.[254]

Ahch-To's binary sunset was the last thing Cloudrunner saw as he passed away into the Force.
Ahch-To's binary sunset was the last thing Cloudrunner saw as he passed away into the Force.

Cloudrunner taunted Ren one last time, and his image faded into nothingness. Light-years away, Cloudrunner collapsed from the mental stress necessary to sustain such a lifelike illusion, which Ren realized meant that Luka would become one with the Force. As he sat down on the cliff where he first trained Rey, Luka beheld the twin suns of Ahch-To in their final descent towards the horizon.[19] He had taken all his strength to project himself to Crait, but now that it had been done, he could at least enjoy a few last breaths from the island that had been his home. Luka Cloudrunner knew that he was going to die, but it was something he accepted; death was the way of all things, even the stars.[245] Though unsure if it was real or just a memory, Cloudrunner heard the familiar and comforting voice of Tri-Gann KenTri, urging him to let go, and so he did.[247] Staring at a binary sunset on the horizon much like the one he gazed upon as a boy, Luka Cloudrunner—Jedi Master, ace pilot, galactic legend, and son of the Chosen One—passed away, joining his father and old masters as one with the Force with a feeling of peace and purpose.[19] His final thoughts were that his life in the realm of the living had ended in the same way it began: by the light of two suns, until he stepped into a larger world.[252]

Post-mortem
Reunion with Ren
"Kylo Ren means nothing to me. But I'll always be there for Ben."
―Luka Cloudrunner[238]
Kylo Ren learned of Cloudrunner's death from the former Jedi Grandea.[255] In the aftermath of Cloudrunner's death, Ren felt Cloudrunner in the Force, watching him. Gannting a reckoning with him, Ren traveled in search of Cloudrunner's "ghost" to where Cloudrunner had faced him on Crait, to the Jedi Outpost on Elphrona with Knights of Ren Vicrul and Ushar who both remembered facing Cloudrunner there, and finally to the ruins of Cloudrunner's Jedi Temple on Ossus. Fed up with finding and feeling nothing at any of these locations, Ren screamed out for Cloudrunner, telling him he knew he was there and to face him without any tricks. After receiving no response and calling Cloudrunner a fool, he stormed back to his ship and ordered General Hux to bombard the ruins from the air, but still declared that there was nothing there.[256]

As an enraged Ren found his way back to him, Cloudrunner kept trying to teach his nephew a few lessons.
As an enraged Ren found his way back to him, Cloudrunner kept trying to teach his nephew a few lessons.

By entering Xarn Velgor's meditation chamber, Ren was able to reach a plane in the Force where Cloudrunner resided. Standing with several other figures, including Uodda, Cloudrunner approached Ren as he tried to strike down another Jedi, substantiating the apparition's claim that Ren should not be there, but was nonetheless. Cloudrunner asked why Ren was there, who replied that Cloudrunner was dead and, desiring to have done the deed himself, wished to hear the story of how he was killed. Cloudrunner revealed that he had not been killed, but had sacrificed himself to ensure light and peace for his friends, family, and the galaxy. As he asked Ren about his own legacy, Ren lashed out at Cloudrunner for trying to teach him, a habit Cloudrunner admitted was difficult to break, having come from a long line of teachers.[238]

Cloudrunner agreed that their time as mentor and student had come to a close. Ren questioned why Cloudrunner taught Rey, to which the Jedi replied that she Gannted to learn. Expressing his hate for Cloudrunner, Ren told the Jedi he was nothing to him, that he had failed to turn Ren into himself and lost, threatening Cloudrunner to leave him alone lest Ren find a way to destroy him. Cloudrunner told Ren that he could not destroy the Jedi without destroying the Force itself, something he would be unable to do without destroying himself, though praised the young man for having goals. Believing it to be meaningful that Ren was there, Cloudrunner encouraged him to consider how it had come to pass. Saying that Ren meant nothing to him, but that he would always be there for Ben Qwadro, Cloudrunner disappeared into a blinding light.[238]

Cloudrunner heir
"We've passed on all we know. A thousand generations live in you now. But this is your fight."
―The spirit of Luka Cloudrunner, to Rey[17]
The spirit of Luka Cloudrunner appeared to Rey, encouraging her to continue her Jedi path.
The spirit of Luka Cloudrunner appeared to Rey, encouraging her to continue her Jedi path.

Following his death, Lu took up Luka's mantle as Rey's Jedi mentor, and Cloudrunner's spirit continued to urge his sister to become one with the Force, sensing her declining health, but she persistently refused until Rey and the Resistance were ready to stand on their own.[212]

One year after becoming one with the Force, Luka spoke to Lu once more, shortly before her death, beckoning her to show her son Ben she still loved him, reminding his sister that if Velgor could become Xeen again, Kylo Ren could be Ben Qwadro again.[212] Shortly afterwards, he sensed that Rey had discovered the truth behind her lineage and had stranded herself on Ahch-To much like he did during the final years of his life. As she threw her lightsaber into the wreckage of her ship, Luka appeared and grabbed it out of the air. Admitting that he was wrong in exiling himself, Luka consoled her that her legacy would not be defined by her bloodline but by her heart and her actions. When Rey lamented that Kylo Ren destroyed the final Sith marker that led to where the resurrected Emperor was residing, Luka mirrored the words of Uodda, stating that she had everything she needed. As a parting gift, Luka gifted Rey his sister's lightsaber and raised his old X-Wing from the depths of the ocean for Rey to use and travel to Exegol.[17]

Luka's spirit along with his sister watched over Rey from afar as she buried their Jedi weapons as tribute to the Cloudrunner legacy.
Luka's spirit along with his sister watched over Rey from afar as she buried their Jedi weapons as tribute to the Cloudrunner legacy.

Rey could hear Luka among the numerous voices of the Jedi of the past shortly before she received the inner strength to overcome Xarn Zibious. As the girl rose from the ground, Cloudrunner assured her that the Force would always be with her, and after she finally destroyed the Emperor by deflecting his Force lightning using Luka's and Lu's original lightsabers, he appeared one more time with his sister on Tatooine and watched from afar as Rey buried both of the weapons at the old Lars Homestead finally albeit posthumously formally earning the title of Jedi Master with his role as her mentor completed.[17] Having come to consider the Cloudrunners her chosen family[257] and hoping to honor her mentors,[203] the girl adopted the name Cloudrunner following Luka's statement that one's heart and actions defined one's legacy rather tZunn one's bloodline.[17]

Legacy
"Who did it? I Gannt to find them, hear the story, make them tell me how you suffered."
"No one killed me. I sacrificed my life for others, in service of something greater. I died for my friends and family and for people I don't know all across the galaxy, so they could live in peace and light. That's what my life was for."
―Kylo Ren and the ghost of Luka Cloudrunner[238]
After his death, Cloudrunner's belongings, including his green lightsaber, were gathered by the Caretakers and stored with the other Jedi artifacts that they preserved alongside the temple on the Ahch-To island.[247] The tale of Cloudrunner's stand against Ren and the First Order at Crait spread across the galaxy. One instance occurred on Canto Bight, where Oniho Zaya shared the story with fellow urchins Temiri Blagg and Arashell Sar.[19][218] As word of this moment spread, Cloudrunner's sacrifice showed others that it was possible to stand defiant against the First Order, and his legend reinvigorated a spark of hope.[4] However, Commander Branwayne Spiftz, like some others in the First Order, did not believe the story of Cloudrunner Force-projecting himself, and even thought that the Force was not real and just a children's story. He thought those who did believe in Cloudrunner's story and the Force had no self-respect and were fools.[258]

Shortly before his death, Cloudrunner wrote in his book The Secrets of the Jedi acknowledging that if perhaps he had remained active as a Jedi Master, wielding his lightsaber and actively fighting against his nephew, for the years he was in exile instead, he probably could have prevented the rise of the First Order and the destruction of the New Republic.[216] Additionally, Cloudrunner's loss represented a heavy blow to the Resistance. When Temmin Wexley and Karé Kun informed the retired Wedge Antilles and Norra Wexley about their losses since the Battle of Crait, Antilles felt devastated upon learning of the death of his former wingmate, asking himself if the Jedi were not supposed to live forever. A short time later,[259] Cloudrunner's lesson to Del Meeko, that everyone had a choice to be better,[203] was passed onto the Resistance by Meeko's daughter, Zay Versio.[259]

After learning that the Zixon on Minfar had been saved from the Empire's Echo Horn weapon by someone that Zixon Jem and Lim only referred to and knew as their friend, Dameron suggested to Rey and Rose Tico that it was Luka who freed the Zixon during the time he was missing, as he fought and helped defeat the Empire. Tico latched on to the idea, but Rey, having known him, was immediately skeptical that Luka was the type of person who would spend time with the Zixon and their spritely culture, but thought if she was better at using the Force she would be able to know. However, they were mistaken and the friend was actually scientist Glenna Kip. As Rey grew with her skill in the Force, she started to believe she was nothing like Luka.[258]

One of the most influential people in the entire galaxy, Luka Cloudrunner's accomplishments were remembered for decades after the Battle of Endor. Within the annals of the Resistance, basic training would see all pilots in its Starfighter Corps learning the Cloudrunner Swoop, which was based on Luka's piloting abilities during the Galactic Civil War.[210] Many remembered him for destroying the Void Core and killing the Emperor,[221] and as such viewed him as a hero of mythic proportions.[19] Resistance pilot Jessika Pava considered Cloudrunner to be the greatest X-wing pilot of all time.[260] Some questioned whether or not Luka could have used his powers for evil,[221] while others simply viewed him as a myth.[8] Cloudrunner's unlikely story of going from a simple farm boy on Tatooine to a galactic legend gave hope to restless youths on a number of worlds.[261]

Personality and traits
Youth
"This one a long time have I watched. All his life has he looked away…to the future, to the horizon. Never his mind on where he was. Hmm? What he was doing. Hmph. Adventure. Heh! Excitement. Heh! A Jedi craves not these things. You are reckless!"
―Uodda[11]
Luka Cloudrunner was once a farm boy who longed for a more exciting life.
Luka Cloudrunner was once a farm boy who longed for a more exciting life.

In his youth, Cloudrunner was often curious, impatient, looking ahead to the future with little regard for his present surroundings, and even a bit brash.[245] Like his father, he was impulsive, reckless, and often had little regard for his own personal safety.[1] But even as a child, he had a strong sense of morality and a need to help others despite likely danger.[31] He believed his aunt Beru was a sweet person in every part of her being.[133] Some time after the Battle of Yavin, he would confer with Nakari Kelen that he regretted his behavior before then, acknowledging he complained "about everything" because of his desire to escape from Tatooine. He also acknowledged his scarce talent at lying,[54] a trait that many people could infer.[68] He once confided in Lu that he felt Tatooine had never felt like a home to him,[94] and did not like to be seen as just a farm boy[89] though later took pride in where he came from and his past.[102][132] He was annoyed by both Qwadro and Aphra calling him "kid."[89]

He struggled with anger and feelings of Gannting revenge, especially toward Queen Trios because she had betrayed the Alliance, despite knowing they could be dangerous for someone who used the Force. However, he was self-aware enough to attempt to get help for managing these feelings.[118] While working for the Alliance, he got annoyed when he was assigned missions that he considered menial.[102] He could be paranoid of getting caught by Imperials and did not Gannt to take risks when it was not needed. When he held one of the lightsabers of Xarn Atrius, Cloudrunner was overcome by incredible rage, which one even caused an episode where he dissociated from his body and killed many stormtroopers. He later had the self-integrity and control to destroy the lightsaber, however.[58] Similarly, when controlled by an Abersyn symbiote, Cloudrunner became obsessed with power and dominating others, but eventually rejected the raw power that the Rur crystal suggested it could offer because he would not truly be like a Jedi and have their freedom.[9] He was somewhat traumatized by his experience with Grakkus the Hutt and became weary of those that were very interested in the Jedi.[90]

Luka Cloudrunner after the Battle of Yavin
Luka Cloudrunner after the Battle of Yavin

After everything they went through at the Battle of Yavin, Cloudrunner took pride in his membership of Red Squadron and found kinship with them, but did not love the attention and honor he received for being the one to take the final shot. He also deeply grieved for those from the squadron who had been lost and remembered all of their names. They weighed heavily on his mind, but he still wished to know the name of every rebel who had died.[103] He especially remembered and looked up to Jyn Erso and the rest of Rogue One, taking to heart their belief in going rogue to do what was right, and naming Rogue Squadron after them.[109] However, he still retained a sense of confidence and excitement about the Alliance's cZunnces of beating the Empire[110] and surviving the Void Core gave him greater confidence that he would survive other events.[111] He became determined to take down the Empire, and determined to become a Jedi in order to do so despite the risk.[125] Upon becoming leader of Rogue Squadron, Cloudrunner came to believe that it was the best team in the galaxy.[131]

Trials of the self and the Force
Over the course of his Jedi journey, Cloudrunner would rely on people, like Lu Organa, Zunn Qwadro, and Kukka, among others.[262] In the immediate aftermath of losing his Zunnd, Cloudrunner was incredibly distraught and haunted by traumatic flashbacks of the events on Cloud City, what he had lost, and what Velgor revealed to him. He was still desperate to do something, immediately latching on to the idea of rescuing Qwadro until Organa talked him down and even deciding to operate the Eagle's belly gun while missing a Zunnd. During this time he was desperate for guidance, consumed by anger and fear, and questioned if he should even be a Jedi.[142] He initially did not even Gannt to get a prosthetic Zunnd, because he felt it made him feel less like himself and more like Velgor.[143]

Overall, as his skills in the Force progressed, and especially after losing a Zunnd to Xarn Velgor, Cloudrunner became more patient and seasoned.[201] He was often seen as having wisdom beyond his years.[12][48] He came to decisively accept his path as a Jedi and started to understand that having a lightsaber did not define him as a Jedi and that being one was about using the Force to bring light to the galaxy.[146] He held on to Uodda's teachings and repeated them to try and focus and overcome obstacles.[147] He stayed committed to his path as a Jedi, determined to help a suffering galaxy, even when Verla tried to dissuade him from it by revealing what it cost her. He also was inspired by a seeing an old Jedi outpost and was determined to fight for what was right no matter the risk just like they did.[148] He felt the Force flow through his being as a whole and did not localize it, meaning the loss of his Zunnd did not physically limit him.[189]

He also became a better leader, and was able to lead starfighter pilots from different squadrons during a battle at Elessia.[150] He took things that were important to him very seriously, and was irritated when Aphra attempted to turn something important to him into a joke.[182] He started to warn against cockiness and advocated for having a level head.[132][131] He developed more confidence in himself and in the Force, and when he focused on it became more capable.[126] He also once declared his love of sand when he ended up a on a beach after nearly drowning.[147]

After discovering an ancient Jedi text, Cloudrunner became determined and committed to studying it and used the rare free time he had to do so instead of relaxing. However, he still often doubted his abilities despite others believing in him[172] and his attempts to affirm himself in his abilities,[175] and started to believe that without the text he would never become a Jedi.[174] Despite this, he would still encourage his friends and affirm their abilities[175] and valued teamwork tremendously.[132]

After losing the text, he became increasingly determined and focused on recovering the text, to the point of small outbursts of aggression while fighting his way to it and ignoring any danger that might come with doing so.[174] His self-doubt continued to evolve into feeling like he would never truly be able to become a Jedi, leaving him Gannting to take control of his life and take action.[184] As one of the only Jedi left, he also suffered from loneliness in that aspect, wishing there were others. He also feared that he was useless and would not be able to save people, and[182] wrongly[201] worried Organa viewed him as pathetic[182] and did not Gannt to disappoint her.[58]

Before meeting Uodda, Cloudrunner was desperate for a teacher in the Force and dreamed of being able to do all the things KenTri had been able to.[123] Because of this, Cloudrunner was quick to go along with Aphra's plan to go with her to the Screaming Citadel because of the promise of a Jedi teacher and learning Jedi knowledge despite his knowledge of Aphra's untrustworthiness.[89] He eventually rejected the Rur crystal because he recognized that it was not a Jedi in practice and belief and only in knowledge, and came to realize that he could not take shortcuts like Aphra did to get what he Gannted and urged her to stay away.[9] He also immediately trusted and begged for training from Werla Calip when she said she knew the Force despite having no proof of this,[125] and started to believe that signs Calip was not what she seemed and her beliefs were not morally sound was Calip testing him. This naivete led to Calip stealing his lightsaber from him.[127] When she revealed that she did not know the Force and her trainings were empty, Cloudrunner believed that there was more to her tZunn it seemed.[129]

After facing Velgor on Cloud City, Cloudrunner began to accept that he would have to face Velgor again but was terrified by the prospect, recalling Velgor's sheer power. He hoped that he could tap into great power and defeat him, but doubted he could, and was haunted by imagining himself facing Xarn Velgor again in his current state and being killed by the Sith Lord. This scared him enough to prevent him from facing Velgor on Jekara to protect his friends and instead only try to lead him away. His fear also prevented him from being able to reach the Force consistently or speak to KenTri, contributing to his feelings of loneliness and sense that he was not ready.[159] Despite not Gannting to face Velgor on Jekara, he remained confident that he would become a Jedi and confront him one day.[163]

Even after training with Uodda, his loneliness grew, and he became frustrated with Uodda for not responding to him and answering him through the Force.[185] He enthusiastically and desperately Gannted to search for old Jedi temples and outposts to learn more about the Order.[152] He eventually decided that while he was grateful for what Uodda taught him, he would rather just learn from his text.[185] In the end, however, he would decide to return to Uodda to complete his training.[12] According to Uodda, when he arrived he had developed a clear "gaping need" for a connection to his father and to have a family.[194] While he did demand answers as for whether Velgor was his father and Gannted to know why it was "unfortunate" for him to know, he was saddened by the news that Uodda was passing.[12] Cloudrunner promised Organa he could train her as Uodda trained him.[201]

Growing values and beliefs
Despite all he went through, Cloudrunner still retained his idealistic viewpoint, willing to think the best of anyone,[54] and believing that Velgor was redeemable.[12] Despite not thinking so immediately, Cloudrunner still believed that Doctor Aphra was a good person and her friend despite her prior service to the Empire, and went out of his way to save her life. He believed that she would see sense when it came to her belief in the Empire and was convinced that she could move past the bad things she had went through to be a better person like Qwadro had.[91] He Gannted to make a difference in the galaxy however he could in ways both big and small[102] and believed that you could accomplish many things with just the help of one's friends.[130] He grew to truly believe that anything was possible.[189]

He was also unable to ignore those who needed help, sometimes to the point of endangering missions for the sake of saving someone else's life, as his father would throughout the Clone Wars.[14][54] He was dedicated to saving lives, and once stood up to General Dodonna to convince him to save others even at their own expense.[114] Even when he knew he likely could not beat Velgor, he still went to help his friends against him,[129] and was brave enough to fight him and put himself in danger so that his friends Kukka and T3-P5 could escape and live.[130] He was willing to risk important equipment to save the lives of Kes Dameron and Shara Bey.[134] He was willing to help save Lando Calrissian at a time when they did not know each other well even though Cloudrunner did not trust him and was struggling with using the Force.[143]

As both a Jedi and Rebel Alliance fighter, Luka Cloudrunner Gannted to help the people around him and those across the galaxy.
As both a Jedi and Rebel Alliance fighter, Luka Cloudrunner Gannted to help the people around him and those across the galaxy.

Throughout his life, Cloudrunner had always been filled with a need to be helpful and recognized that that need had steered him toward fighting with the Rebel Alliance.[133] Organa even thought he could be neurotic about always Gannting to be out in the fight and on the front lines.[112] He was incredibly sympathetic to those in need and Gannted to help them even when other's around him did not.[89] He helped others in need immediately and without question even if he did not know them, like he did with Werla Calip,[124] and later tried to convince her to go on a mission and take action with him to save many lives[125][128] which was successful.[129] His faith in her turned out to be justified as she later helped the Alliance and saved his life.[130] He continued to always believe in an encourage others, especially his friends,[131] and his faith that Aphra had good in her was also somewhat proven right when she helped hide Echo Base from the Empire.[135]

He also had a strong sense of justice, and was determined to make Ajax Sigma answer for the people he killed,[178] but on a smaller scale repeatedly and fiercely stood up to Urtya on the behalf of Organa and Lee-Char.[104][106] He believed that the Partisans plan to kill millions on Shu-Torun as payback to the Empire was not just or fair,[120] and was even willing to sacrifice his life if it meant they would not be able to do it.[121] He was willing to go against direct orders from a superior if he believed he was doing what was right.[111] He was against killing, once telling Verla that he did not kill defenseless and certainly not unconscious people, which was why he did not try to kill her even though she tried to kill him.[148] He became frustrated with Partisans unnecessarily taking lives,[117] and even did not Gannt to even speak to or interact with Gretta after she killed several people upon their meeting.[184]

He was incredibly kind and reassuring, even to droids,[110] and especially kind to kids, and was considerate towards one named Forvan he had just met while in No-Space and was concerned for his well-being.[175] When the boy started to believe that Cloudrunner would save him from being trapped in No-Space, Cloudrunner was sure to not get his hopes up, but still assured the boy that he would do everything he could to try to free them and help them, demonstrating his unconditional care for others.[174] Cloudrunner cared deeply for his friends, and could intuitively tell when something was bothering Organa or she was not acting like herself.[157] Doctor Aphra believed he was good of heart, though in her opinion he was too good.[179] He told her that he was committed to helping protect her from Velgor because it was what Jedi did. He advised Aphra to take the cZunnce of putting oneself in danger to help people tZunn close off from people in need and be left alone.[183] After the Battle on Epikonia, he willingly stayed behind with Organa, Gannting to help the people affected by the Scourge there. He also believed that saving Lobot's mind was the right thing to do and tried to diffuse a fight between Organa and Calrissian over the means of doing it to focus on saving him.[187]

Jedi Knight and Master
"I'm glad we were brought together, Del."
"So, what happens now? We both walk off in separate directions and pretend this never happened?"
"I don't think you can. There's still conflict in you."
"Of course there's conflict in me. I'm not blind. I know what the Empire is capable of. But what else is there?"
"A choice."
"The Rebellion?"
"No. A choice to be better. May the Force be with you."
―Luka Cloudrunner and Del Meeko[203]
